	[2025-12-02 18:20:44,97] [info] BackgroundConfigAsyncJobExecutionActor [[38;5;2mb3766e0b[0mUnmappedBamToAlignedBam.CreateSequenceGroupingTSV:NA:1]: [38;5;5mpython3 <<CODE
	with open("/cromwell-executions/WholeGenomeGermlineSingleSample/0c205b5c-e940-4d91-84d8-a29a2e6929b0/call-UnmappedBamToAlignedBam/UnmappedBamToAlignedBam/b3766e0b-927e-4e59-a50b-50ba61a28af6/call-CreateSequenceGroupingTSV/inputs/-1569105944/Homo_sapiens_assembly38_masked.dict", "r") as ref_dict_file:
	    sequence_tuple_list = []
	    longest_sequence = 0
	    for line in ref_dict_file:
	        if line.startswith("@SQ"):
	            line_split = line.split("\t")
	            # (Sequence_Name, Sequence_Length)
	            sequence_tuple_list.append((line_split[1].split("SN:")[1], int(line_split[2].split("LN:")[1])))
	    longest_sequence = sorted(sequence_tuple_list, key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)[0][1]
	# We are adding this to the intervals because hg38 has contigs named with embedded colons and a bug in GATK strips off
	# the last element after a :, so we add this as a sacrificial element.
	hg38_protection_tag = ":1+"
	# initialize the tsv string with the first sequence
	tsv_string = sequence_tuple_list[0][0] + hg38_protection_tag
	temp_size = sequence_tuple_list[0][1]
	for sequence_tuple in sequence_tuple_list[1:]:
	    if temp_size + sequence_tuple[1] <= longest_sequence:
	        temp_size += sequence_tuple[1]
	        tsv_string += "\t" + sequence_tuple[0] + hg38_protection_tag
	    else:
	        tsv_string += "\n" + sequence_tuple[0] + hg38_protection_tag
	        temp_size = sequence_tuple[1]
	# add the unmapped sequences as a separate line to ensure that they are recalibrated as well
	with open("sequence_grouping.txt","w") as tsv_file:
	  tsv_file.write(tsv_string)
	  tsv_file.close()
	
	tsv_string += '\n' + "unmapped"
	
	with open("sequence_grouping_with_unmapped.txt","w") as tsv_file_with_unmapped:
	  tsv_file_with_unmapped.write(tsv_string)
	  tsv_file_with_unmapped.close()
	CODE[0m

	import csv
	import sys
	with open('sorted-ERR3687419_ILMN.preBqsr.selfSM') as selfSM:
	  reader = csv.DictReader(selfSM, delimiter='\t')
	  i = 0
	  for row in reader:
	    if float(row["FREELK0"])==0 and float(row["FREELK1"])==0:
	      # a zero value for the likelihoods implies no data. This usually indicates a problem rather than a real event.
	      # if the bam isn't really empty, this is probably due to the use of a incompatible reference build between
	      # vcf and bam.
	      sys.stderr.write("Found zero likelihoods. Bam is either very-very shallow, or aligned to the wrong reference (relative to the vcf).")
	      sys.exit(1)
	    print(float(row["FREEMIX"])/0.75)
	    i = i + 1
	    # there should be exactly one row, and if this isn't the case the format of the output is unexpectedly different
	    # and the results are not reliable.
	    if i != 1:
	      sys.stderr.write("Found %d rows in .selfSM file. Was expecting exactly 1. This is an error"%(i))
	      sys.exit(2)
	CODE[0m

	# Works around a JES limitation where multiples files with the same name overwrite each other when globbed

	for i, interval in enumerate(intervals):
	  (directory, filename) = os.path.split(interval)

	# We need at least 1 GB of available memory outside of the Java heap in order to execute native code, thus, limit
	# Java's memory by the total memory minus 1 GB. We need to compute the total memory as it might differ from
	# memory_size_gb because of Cromwell's retry with more memory feature.
	mem_unit=$(echo "${MEM_UNIT:-}" | cut -c 1)
	
	if [[ $mem_unit == "M" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE)}")
	elif [[ $mem_unit == "G" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE * 1024)}")
	else
	  available_memory_mb=$(free -m | awk '/^Mem/ {print $2}')
	fi
	
	let java_memory_size_mb=available_memory_mb-1024
	echo Total available memory: ${available_memory_mb} MB >&2
	echo Memory reserved for Java: ${java_memory_size_mb} MB >&2
	
	gatk --java-options "-Xmx150000m -Xms150000m -XX:GCTimeLimit=50 -XX:GCHeapFreeLimit=10" \

	# We need at least 1 GB of available memory outside of the Java heap in order to execute native code, thus, limit
	# Java's memory by the total memory minus 1 GB. We need to compute the total memory as it might differ from
	# memory_size_gb because of Cromwell's retry with more memory feature.
	mem_unit=$(echo "${MEM_UNIT:-}" | cut -c 1)
	
	if [[ $mem_unit == "M" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE)}")
	elif [[ $mem_unit == "G" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE * 1024)}")
	else
	  available_memory_mb=$(free -m | awk '/^Mem/ {print $2}')
	fi
	
	let java_memory_size_mb=available_memory_mb-1024
	echo Total available memory: ${available_memory_mb} MB >&2
	echo Memory reserved for Java: ${java_memory_size_mb} MB >&2

	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE)}")

	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE * 1024)}")

	  available_memory_mb=$(free -m | awk '/^Mem/ {print $2}')

	# We need at least 1 GB of available memory outside of the Java heap in order to execute native code, thus, limit
	# Java's memory by the total memory minus 1 GB. We need to compute the total memory as it might differ from
	# memory_size_gb because of Cromwell's retry with more memory feature.
	mem_unit=$(echo "${MEM_UNIT:-}" | cut -c 1)
	
	if [[ $mem_unit == "M" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE)}")
	elif [[ $mem_unit == "G" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE * 1024)}")
	else
	  available_memory_mb=$(free -m | awk '/^Mem/ {print $2}')
	fi
	
	let java_memory_size_mb=available_memory_mb-1024
	echo Total available memory: ${available_memory_mb} MB >&2
	echo Memory reserved for Java: ${java_memory_size_mb} MB >&2

	# We need at least 1 GB of available memory outside of the Java heap in order to execute native code, thus, limit
	# Java's memory by the total memory minus 1 GB. We need to compute the total memory as it might differ from
	# memory_size_gb because of Cromwell's retry with more memory feature.
	mem_unit=$(echo "${MEM_UNIT:-}" | cut -c 1)
	
	if [[ $mem_unit == "M" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE)}")
	elif [[ $mem_unit == "G" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE * 1024)}")
	else
	  available_memory_mb=$(free -m | awk '/^Mem/ {print $2}')
	fi
	
	let java_memory_size_mb=available_memory_mb-1024
	echo Total available memory: ${available_memory_mb} MB >&2
	echo Memory reserved for Java: ${java_memory_size_mb} MB >&2

	# Java's memory by the total memory minus 1 GB. We need to compute the total memory as it might differ from

	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE)}")
	elif [[ $mem_unit == "G" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE * 1024)}")
	else
	  available_memory_mb=$(free -m | awk '/^Mem/ {print $2}')
	fi

	# We need at least 1 GB of available memory outside of the Java heap in order to execute native code, thus, limit
	# Java's memory by the total memory minus 1 GB. We need to compute the total memory as it might differ from
	# memory_size_gb because of Cromwell's retry with more memory feature.
	mem_unit=$(echo "${MEM_UNIT:-}" | cut -c 1)
	
	if [[ $mem_unit == "M" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE)}")
	elif [[ $mem_unit == "G" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE * 1024)}")
	else
	  available_memory_mb=$(free -m | awk '/^Mem/ {print $2}')
	fi
	
	let java_memory_size_mb=available_memory_mb-1024
	echo Total available memory: ${available_memory_mb} MB >&2
	echo Memory reserved for Java: ${java_memory_size_mb} MB >&2

	# We need at least 1 GB of available memory outside of the Java heap in order to execute native code, thus, limit
	# Java's memory by the total memory minus 1 GB. We need to compute the total memory as it might differ from
	# memory_size_gb because of Cromwell's retry with more memory feature.
	mem_unit=$(echo "${MEM_UNIT:-}" | cut -c 1)
	
	if [[ $mem_unit == "M" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE)}")
	elif [[ $mem_unit == "G" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE * 1024)}")
	else
	  available_memory_mb=$(free -m | awk '/^Mem/ {print $2}')
	fi
	
	let java_memory_size_mb=available_memory_mb-1024
	echo Total available memory: ${available_memory_mb} MB >&2
	echo Memory reserved for Java: ${java_memory_size_mb} MB >&2

	# Java's memory by the total memory minus 1 GB. We need to compute the total memory as it might differ from
	# memory_size_gb because of Cromwell's retry with more memory feature.
	mem_unit=$(echo "${MEM_UNIT:-}" | cut -c 1)
	
	if [[ $mem_unit == "M" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE)}")
	elif [[ $mem_unit == "G" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE * 1024)}")
	else
	  available_memory_mb=$(free -m | awk '/^Mem/ {print $2}')
	fi
	
	let java_memory_size_mb=available_memory_mb-1024

	echo Memory reserved for Java: ${java_memory_size_mb} MB >&2

	# We need at least 1 GB of available memory outside of the Java heap in order to execute native code, thus, limit
	# Java's memory by the total memory minus 1 GB. We need to compute the total memory as it might differ from
	# memory_size_gb because of Cromwell's retry with more memory feature.

	if [[ $mem_unit == "M" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE)}")
	elif [[ $mem_unit == "G" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE * 1024)}")
	else
	  available_memory_mb=$(free -m | awk '/^Mem/ {print $2}')
	fi
	
	let java_memory_size_mb=available_memory_mb-1024

	echo Memory reserved for Java: ${java_memory_size_mb} MB >&2

	# We need at least 1 GB of available memory outside of the Java heap in order to execute native code, thus, limit
	# Java's memory by the total memory minus 1 GB. We need to compute the total memory as it might differ from
	# memory_size_gb because of Cromwell's retry with more memory feature.
	mem_unit=$(echo "${MEM_UNIT:-}" | cut -c 1)
	
	if [[ $mem_unit == "M" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE)}")
	elif [[ $mem_unit == "G" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE * 1024)}")
	else
	  available_memory_mb=$(free -m | awk '/^Mem/ {print $2}')
	fi
	
	let java_memory_size_mb=available_memory_mb-1024
	echo Total available memory: ${available_memory_mb} MB >&2
	echo Memory reserved for Java: ${java_memory_size_mb} MB >&2

	[2025-12-04 21:37:03,68] [info] BackgroundConfigAsyncJobExecutionActor [[38;5;2m4f5e5eb8[0mVariantCalling.HaplotypeCallerGATK4:8:1]: [38;5;5mset -e
	
	# We need at least 1 GB of available memory outside of the Java heap in order to execute native code, thus, limit
	# Java's memory by the total memory minus 1 GB. We need to compute the total memory as it might differ from
	# memory_size_gb because of Cromwell's retry with more memory feature.
	mem_unit=$(echo "${MEM_UNIT:-}" | cut -c 1)
	
	if [[ $mem_unit == "M" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE)}")
	elif [[ $mem_unit == "G" ]]; then
	  available_memory_mb=$(awk "BEGIN {print int($MEM_SIZE * 1024)}")
	else
	  available_memory_mb=$(free -m | awk '/^Mem/ {print $2}')
	fi
	
	let java_memory_size_mb=available_memory_mb-1024
	echo Total available memory: ${available_memory_mb} MB >&2
	echo Memory reserved for Java: ${java_memory_size_mb} MB >&2
	
	gatk --java-options "-Xmx150000m -Xms150000m -XX:GCTimeLimit=50 -XX:GCHeapFreeLimit=10" \
	  HaplotypeCaller \
	  -R /cromwell-executions/WholeGenomeGermlineSingleSample/0c205b5c-e940-4d91-84d8-a29a2e6929b0/call-BamToGvcf/VariantCalling/4f5e5eb8-6060-4966-8ad6-2fe9507d7b42/call-HaplotypeCallerGATK4/shard-8/inputs/-1569105944/Homo_sapiens_assembly38_masked.fasta \
	  -I /cromwell-executions/WholeGenomeGermlineSingleSample/0c205b5c-e940-4d91-84d8-a29a2e6929b0/call-BamToGvcf/VariantCalling/4f5e5eb8-6060-4966-8ad6-2fe9507d7b42/call-HaplotypeCallerGATK4/shard-8/inputs/356907427/sorted-ERR3687419_ILMN.bam \
